## WARNING · numerical · `initialization_near_constraint_boundary`

111 inequality constraint row(s) start within the active-set tolerance of a finite boundary.

**Why it matters:** Boundary starts can immediately create an active set, leave little feasibility-restoration room, and make solver behavior sensitive to initialization perturbations.

**Evidence basis:** local_inference; **confidence:** high.

### Evidence

- Numerical evaluation point
  - `label`: initialization
  - `provenance_kind`: UserPoint

**Why it matters:** Agreement between independent dense singular-value and sparse pivot screens strengthens this local numerical observation, without proving exact rank.

**Evidence basis:** numerical_observation; **confidence:** medium.
